Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a population algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 87. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 108/1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to population structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 87/108 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the population structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 553. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 890 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on population structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 553. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 890. QED.
